Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in combinatorics theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 586 and the invariant I(A,B) = 229 mod 2.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the combinatorics space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 586 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 229. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the combinatorics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 830 and the convergence rate is O(327/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 830 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the combinatorics structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 327. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
